### Task 10: End-to-end suite on the scripted node

**Files:**
- Create (test support): `test/adapters/tron/support/env.ts`
- Test: `test/adapters/tron/e2e.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: everything above through the public API: `CryptoAio`, `Blockchain`, `native`, `FaultyOperationStore`, `CrashError`, `MemoryOperationStore`, `ScriptedTronNode`; `fenceGeneration` from `src/testing/generation.ts` (Plan 2 Task 11, ruling A5).
- Produces (test support): `countingSigner(): { signer, calls() }` (the test key, counting `sign` calls); `createTronEnv({ network?, endpoints?, node?, fund?, stores?, signer?, indexer?, options?, lifecycle? })` returning `{ aio, bc, node, clock, stores, address, run, mineWhile, restart }`; `mineWhile(promise, { maxBlocks?, include? })` mines one block per 3 fake seconds; `restart({ killPrevious? })` kills the old generation through `fenceGeneration` and builds a new container over the same stores, node and clock (handoff R20, D15).

No production code changes in this task: the suite proves the tasks above work together through the engine, the monitor and the scanner. It covers a TRX transfer to proven finality at the solidified block; account creation and the memo fee with the memo decoded back; a USDT transfer with an evidenced `Transfer`; `OUT_OF_ENERGY` after dynamic energy rose, proven `failed`; a token that logs nothing, never `executed`; a never-activated sender refused before signing; a transaction that never lands, proven `expired`, refused `replace`, then `rebuild` to finality, signing exactly twice; a reorg of an unsolidified block; crash recovery with the process killed after signing and after broadcasting, signing once; deposit scanning with a junk token marked unresolved (R35); the handle's own native `TronWeb`; a fee-on-transfer token counted as executed with the amounts that moved reported (F5); and one Operation for a recipient given in base58 and in hex under one key (F11). `fund: 0n` leaves the sender never activated (F6).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the test support**

`test/adapters/tron/support/env.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * A container on the scripted Tron node, for end-to-end tests. `restart({ killPrevious:
 * true })` kills the old generation through `fenceGeneration` (handoff R20, ruling A5): every
 * clock sleep, fetch, store and signer call of the old container then never settles, exactly
 * like a dead process. A plain `restart()` starts a second live generation.
 */
import {
  CryptoAio,
  noopLogger,
  type Blockchain,
  type LifecycleOptions,
  type Signer,
  type Stores,
} from '../../../../src';
import { secret } from '../../../../src/core/secret/secret';
import { localSigner } from '../../../../src/core/signing/local';
import { createMemoryStores } from '../../../../src/core/store/memory';
import { FakeClock, drive } from '../../../../src/testing/fake-clock';
import { fenceGeneration } from '../../../../src/testing/generation';
import { ScriptedTronNode, type NodeOptions } from './node';
import { KEY, KEY_ADDRESS } from './vectors';

export interface TronEnvOptions {
  readonly network?: 'mainnet' | 'shasta' | 'nile';
  readonly endpoints?: readonly string[];
  readonly node?: Partial<Omit<NodeOptions, 'clock' | 'network'>>;
  /** Sun for the test key's account (default 1,000 TRX). */
  readonly fund?: bigint;
  readonly stores?: Partial<Stores>;
  readonly signer?: Signer;
  readonly indexer?: boolean;
  readonly options?: Readonly<Record<string, unknown>>;
  readonly lifecycle?: LifecycleOptions;
}

/** A local signer holding the test key that counts its `sign` calls. */
export function countingSigner(): { readonly signer: Signer; calls(): number } {
  const inner = localSigner({ id: 'hot', secp256k1: secret(KEY) });
  let calls = 0;
  const signer: Signer = {
    id: inner.id,
    schemes: inner.schemes,
    getPublicKey: (scheme, keyRef) => inner.getPublicKey(scheme, keyRef),
    sign: (requests, ctx) => {
      calls += 1;
      return inner.sign(requests, ctx);
    },
  };
  return { signer, calls: () => calls };
}

export function createTronEnv(options: TronEnvOptions = {}) {
  const network = options.network ?? 'nile';
  const clock = new FakeClock(1_790_000_000_000);
  const node = new ScriptedTronNode({ solidDepth: 3, ...options.node, network, clock });
  const endpoints = (options.endpoints ?? ['main']).map((name) => ({
    name,
    url: node.endpoint(name),
  }));
  const signer = options.signer ?? countingSigner().signer;
  const stores: Stores = { ...createMemoryStores(clock), ...options.stores };
  // `fund: 0n` leaves the test key's account never activated (no account on chain).
  const fund = options.fund ?? 1_000_000_000n;
  if (fund > 0n) node.fund(KEY_ADDRESS, fund);

  let generation = { alive: true };
  const assemble = () => {
    const fenced = fenceGeneration(
      { clock, fetch: node.fetch.fetch, stores, signers: { hot: signer } },
      generation,
    );
    const aio = new CryptoAio({
      env: false,
      logger: noopLogger,
      clock: fenced.clock,
      stores: fenced.stores,
      transport: { fetch: fenced.fetch, baseDelayMs: 1, maxDelayMs: 5, timeoutMs: 5_000 },
      providers: { node: { endpoints } },
      signers: fenced.signers,
      wallets: { main: { signer: 'hot' } },
      chains: {
        tron: {
          network,
          provider: 'node',
          ...(options.indexer ? { indexer: 'node' } : {}),
          wallet: 'main',
          ...(options.options ? { options: options.options } : {}),
        },
      },
      lifecycle: {
        pollIntervalMs: 3_000,
        droppedGracePeriodMs: 30_000,
        rebroadcastIntervalMs: 15_000,
        waitTimeoutMs: 1_800_000,
        leaseMs: 30_000,
        claimLeaseMs: 30_000,
        ...options.lifecycle,
      },
    });
    const bc = aio.blockchain({ chain: 'tron' }) as Blockchain<'tron'>;
    return { aio, bc };
  };

  const run = <T>(promise: Promise<T>, stepMs = 100): Promise<T> =>
    drive(clock, promise, stepMs);
  /** Mines one block per 3 fake seconds until `promise` settles. */
  const mineWhile = async <T>(
    promise: Promise<T>,
    options: { readonly maxBlocks?: number; readonly include?: boolean } = {},
  ): Promise<T> => {
    let done = false;
    const tracked = promise.finally(() => {
      done = true;
    });
    tracked.catch(() => undefined);
    for (let i = 0; i < (options.maxBlocks ?? 600) && !done; i++) {
      await clock.advance(3_000);
      node.mine(options.include === false ? { include: false } : {});
    }
    return tracked;
  };
  let current = assemble();
  const restart = async (restartOptions: { readonly killPrevious?: boolean } = {}) => {
    if (restartOptions.killPrevious) generation.alive = false;
    generation = { alive: true };
    current = assemble();
    return current;
  };
  return {
    get aio() {
      return current.aio;
    },
    get bc() {
      return current.bc;
    },
    node,
    clock,
    stores,
    address: KEY_ADDRESS,
    run,
    mineWhile,
    restart,
  };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the suite**

`test/adapters/tron/e2e.test.ts`:

```ts
import type { OperationPatch, ScanEvent } from '../../../src';
import { MemoryOperationStore } from '../../../src/core/store/memory';
import { native } from '../../../src/native';
import { CrashError, FaultyOperationStore } from '../../../src/testing/faulty-store';
import { encodeTransfer } from '../../../src/adapters/tron/abi';
import { toBase58Address, toHexAddress } from '../../../src/adapters/tron/address';
import { countingSigner, createTronEnv } from './support/env';
import { FEE_COLLECTOR } from './support/node';
import { signedTransaction } from './support/signing';
import { KEY_HEX, RECIPIENT, USDT } from './support/vectors';

const TOKEN = { standard: 'trc20', contract: USDT } as const;
const JUNK = 'TXYZopYRdj2D9XRtbG411XZZ3kM5VkAeBf';

// `update(namespace, id, patch, …)`: the patch is the third argument.
const patchState = (state: string) => (args: readonly unknown[]) =>
  (args[2] as OperationPatch | undefined)?.state === state;

jest.setTimeout(60_000);

function withToken(
  env: ReturnType<typeof createTronEnv>,
  holder = env.address,
  amount = 1_000_000_000n,
) {
  env.node.deployToken(USDT, { symbol: 'USDT', decimals: 6 });
  env.node.mintToken(USDT, holder, amount);
}

describe('Tron end to end', () => {
  it('runs a TRX transfer to proven finality at the solidified block', async () => {
    const env = createTronEnv();
    const sub = await env.run(
      env.bc.transfer({ to: RECIPIENT, amount: '1.5' }, { idempotencyKey: 't1' }),
    );
    expect(sub).toMatchObject({
      state: 'submitted',
      attempt: { idKind: 'tx-hash', canonical: true },
    });
    expect(env.node.inPool(sub.attempt?.id ?? '')).toBe(true);
    const final = await env.mineWhile(sub.wait({ finality: 'final' }));
    expect(final.status).toMatchObject({
      state: 'final',
      evidence: 'proven',
      finality: 'final',
    });
    expect(env.node.balance(RECIPIENT)).toBe(1_500_000n);
    const tx = await env.run(env.bc.getTransaction(sub.attempt?.id ?? ''));
    expect(tx?.transfers[0]).toMatchObject({
      to: { canonical: RECIPIENT },
      amount: { base: 1_500_000n },
    });
    expect(tx?.decoding).toBe('complete');
  });

  it('charges account creation and the memo fee, and decodes the memo', async () => {
    const env = createTronEnv();
    const fee = await env.run(
      env.bc.estimateFee({ to: RECIPIENT, amount: 1n, memo: 'order 7' }),
    );
    expect(fee.charges.map((c) => [c.label, c.amount.base])).toEqual([
      ['bandwidth', 100_000n],
      ['activation', 1_000_000n],
      ['memo', 1_000_000n],
    ]);
    const sub = await env.run(
      env.bc.transfer({ to: RECIPIENT, amount: 1n, memo: 'order 7' }),
    );
    await env.mineWhile(sub.wait({ finality: 'final' }));
    const tx = await env.run(env.bc.getTransaction(sub.attempt?.id ?? ''));
    expect(tx?.transfers[0]).toMatchObject({ memo: 'order 7' });
    expect(env.node.exists(RECIPIENT)).toBe(true);
  });

  it('transfers USDT with an evidenced Transfer event', async () => {
    const env = createTronEnv();
    withToken(env);
    const sub = await env.run(
      env.bc.transfer({ asset: TOKEN, to: RECIPIENT, amount: '2.5' }),
    );
    const final = await env.mineWhile(sub.wait({ finality: 'final' }));
    expect(final.status.state).toBe('final');
    expect(env.node.tokenBalance(USDT, RECIPIENT)).toBe(2_500_000n);
    const op = await env.run(env.bc.getOperation(sub.operationId));
    expect(op).toMatchObject({ state: 'final', outcome: 'executed' });
  });

  it('proves failed a transfer included with too little energy (OUT_OF_ENERGY)', async () => {
    const env = createTronEnv();
    withToken(env);
    const sub = await env.run(
      env.bc.transfer({ asset: TOKEN, to: RECIPIENT, amount: 1n }),
    );
    env.node.energyFactor = 300n; // dynamic energy rose after the estimate
    await expect(env.mineWhile(sub.wait({ finality: 'final' }))).rejects.toMatchObject({
      code: 'TX_REVERTED',
    });
    const op = await env.run(env.bc.getOperation(sub.operationId));
    expect(op?.state).toBe('failed');
    expect(env.node.tokenBalance(USDT, RECIPIENT)).toBe(0n);
  });

  it('never reports success for a token that moved nothing and logged nothing (lesson 7)', async () => {
    const env = createTronEnv();
    env.node.deployToken(USDT, { symbol: 'USDT', decimals: 6, mode: 'no-log' });
    env.node.mintToken(USDT, env.address, 1_000n);
    const sub = await env.run(
      env.bc.transfer({ asset: TOKEN, to: RECIPIENT, amount: 5n }),
    );
    await expect(env.mineWhile(sub.wait({ finality: 'final' }))).rejects.toMatchObject({
      code: 'TX_REVERTED',
    });
  });

  it('counts a fee-on-transfer token as executed and reports the amounts that moved (F5)', async () => {
    const env = createTronEnv();
    env.node.deployToken(USDT, { symbol: 'USDT', decimals: 6, mode: 'fee' });
    env.node.mintToken(USDT, env.address, 1_000_000n);
    const sub = await env.run(
      env.bc.transfer({ asset: TOKEN, to: RECIPIENT, amount: 100n }),
    );
    const final = await env.mineWhile(sub.wait({ finality: 'final' }));
    expect(final.status.state).toBe('final');
    expect(await env.run(env.bc.getOperation(sub.operationId))).toMatchObject({
      state: 'final',
      outcome: 'executed',
    });
    const tx = await env.run(env.bc.getTransaction(sub.attempt?.id ?? ''));
    expect(tx?.transfers.map((t) => [t.to.canonical, t.amount?.base])).toEqual([
      [RECIPIENT, 99n],
      [toBase58Address(FEE_COLLECTOR), 1n],
    ]);
  });

  it('treats a base58 and a hex recipient as one intent under one idempotency key (F11)', async () => {
    const env = createTronEnv();
    const first = await env.run(
      env.bc.transfer({ to: RECIPIENT, amount: 5n }, { idempotencyKey: 'same' }),
    );
    const again = await env.run(
      env.bc.transfer(
        { to: toHexAddress(RECIPIENT), amount: 5n },
        { idempotencyKey: 'same' },
      ),
    );
    expect(again.operationId).toBe(first.operationId);
  });

  it('fails before signing when the sender was never activated', async () => {
    const { signer, calls } = countingSigner();
    const env = createTronEnv({ fund: 0n, signer });
    expect(env.node.exists(env.address)).toBe(false);
    await expect(
      env.run(env.bc.transfer({ to: RECIPIENT, amount: 1n })),
    ).rejects.toMatchObject({
      code: 'INSUFFICIENT_FUNDS',
    });
    expect(calls()).toBe(0);
  });

  it('proves expiry when the transaction never lands, then rebuilds it on request', async () => {
    const { signer, calls } = countingSigner();
    const env = createTronEnv({ signer });
    const sub = await env.run(
      env.bc.transfer({ to: RECIPIENT, amount: 7n }, { idempotencyKey: 'x1' }),
    );
    // The node keeps it pooled but never includes it; it expires and leaves the pool.
    await expect(
      env.mineWhile(sub.wait({ finality: 'final' }), { include: false, maxBlocks: 60 }),
    ).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'TX_EXPIRED' });
    expect((await env.run(env.bc.getOperation(sub.operationId)))?.state).toBe('expired');
    await expect(
      env.run(env.bc.replace(sub.operationId, { fee: 'fast' })),
    ).rejects.toMatchObject({
      code: 'UNSUPPORTED_CAPABILITY',
    });
    const rebuilt = await env.run(env.bc.rebuild(sub.operationId));
    expect(rebuilt.attempt?.id).not.toBe(sub.attempt?.id);
    await env.mineWhile(rebuilt.wait({ finality: 'final' }));
    expect(env.node.balance(RECIPIENT)).toBe(7n);
    expect(calls()).toBe(2);
  });

  it('follows a reorg of an unsolidified block and still reaches finality', async () => {
    const env = createTronEnv();
    const sub = await env.run(env.bc.transfer({ to: RECIPIENT, amount: 9n }));
    env.node.mine();
    await env.run(sub.wait({ confirmations: 1 }));
    const reorged: unknown[] = [];
    env.aio.on('tx.reorged', (e) => reorged.push(e));
    env.node.reorg(1);
    await env.mineWhile(sub.wait({ finality: 'final' }));
    expect(env.node.balance(RECIPIENT)).toBe(9n);
    expect(reorged.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(1);
  });

  describe('crash and recovery', () => {
    function crashEnv() {
      const faulty = new FaultyOperationStore(new MemoryOperationStore());
      const { signer, calls } = countingSigner();
      const env = createTronEnv({ stores: { operations: faulty }, signer });
      return { env, faulty, calls };
    }

    it('rebroadcasts a signed-but-never-sent transfer after the process died, signing once', async () => {
      const { env, faulty, calls } = crashEnv();
      faulty.crashOn({ method: 'appendAttempt', timing: 'after' });
      await expect(
        env.run(env.bc.transfer({ to: RECIPIENT, amount: 3n }, { idempotencyKey: 'k' })),
      ).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(CrashError);
      const stored = await env.stores.operations.getByKey('default', 'k');
      const ref = stored?.attempts[0]?.ref.id ?? '';
      expect([stored?.state, env.node.inPool(ref)]).toEqual(['signed', false]);
      const restarted = await env.restart({ killPrevious: true });
      const report = await env.run(restarted.aio.operations.recover());
      expect(report).toMatchObject({ rebroadcast: 1, failed: 0 });
      expect(env.node.inPool(ref)).toBe(true);
      const sub = await env.run(
        restarted.bc.transfer({ to: RECIPIENT, amount: 3n }, { idempotencyKey: 'k' }),
      );
      expect([sub.state, sub.attempt?.id, calls()]).toEqual(['submitted', ref, 1]);
      await env.mineWhile(sub.wait({ finality: 'final' }));
      expect(env.node.balance(RECIPIENT)).toBe(3n);
    });

    it('recovers a broadcast that was never recorded: the node answers "duplicate"', async () => {
      const { env, faulty, calls } = crashEnv();
      faulty.crashOn({
        method: 'update',
        timing: 'before',
        when: patchState('submitted'),
      });
      await expect(
        env.run(env.bc.transfer({ to: RECIPIENT, amount: 4n }, { idempotencyKey: 'k' })),
      ).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'STATE_UNRECORDED', ambiguous: true });
      const stored = await env.stores.operations.getByKey('default', 'k');
      const ref = stored?.attempts[0]?.ref.id ?? '';
      expect([stored?.state, env.node.inPool(ref)]).toEqual(['signed', true]);
      const restarted = await env.restart({ killPrevious: true });
      expect(await env.run(restarted.aio.operations.recover())).toMatchObject({
        rebroadcast: 1,
        failed: 0,
      });
      const sub = await env.run(
        restarted.bc.transfer({ to: RECIPIENT, amount: 4n }, { idempotencyKey: 'k' }),
      );
      expect([sub.state, calls()]).toEqual(['submitted', 1]);
      await env.mineWhile(sub.wait({ finality: 'final' }));
      expect(env.node.balance(RECIPIENT)).toBe(4n);
    });
  });

  it('scans final blocks for deposits, marking a junk token unresolved (R35)', async () => {
    const env = createTronEnv();
    withToken(env);
    env.node.deployToken(JUNK, {
      symbol: 'JUNK',
      decimals: 6,
      mode: 'reverting-metadata',
    });
    env.node.mintToken(JUNK, env.address, 100n);
    await env.run(env.bc.transfer({ to: RECIPIENT, amount: 4n }));
    await env.run(env.bc.transfer({ asset: TOKEN, to: RECIPIENT, amount: 2n }));
    // The handle refuses the junk token (its metadata fails), so send it as a third party would.
    await expect(
      env.run(
        env.bc.transfer({
          asset: { standard: 'trc20', contract: JUNK },
          to: RECIPIENT,
          amount: 5n,
        }),
      ),
    ).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'ASSET_RESOLUTION' });
    const head = env.node.block(env.node.head) as { id: string; timestamp: number };
    const junk = signedTransaction({
      refBlockBytes: head.id.slice(12, 16),
      refBlockHash: head.id.slice(16, 32),
      expiration: head.timestamp + 60_000,
      timestamp: head.timestamp + 7,
      feeLimit: 100_000_000,
      contract: {
        type: 'TriggerSmartContract',
        owner: KEY_HEX,
        contract: toHexAddress(JUNK),
        data: encodeTransfer(RECIPIENT, 5n),
      },
    });
    await env.run(
      env.node.fetch.fetch('https://main.tron.test/wallet/broadcasthex', {
        method: 'POST',
        body: JSON.stringify({ transaction: junk.hex }),
      }),
    );
    const scanner = env.bc
      .scanner({
        cursorKey: 'deposits',
        from: 0n,
        mode: 'final',
        filter: { addresses: [RECIPIENT] },
      })
      [Symbol.asyncIterator]();
    const seen: ScanEvent[] = [];
    for (
      let i = 0;
      i < 200 &&
      seen.flatMap((e) => (e.type === 'block' ? e.transactions : [])).length < 3;
      i++
    ) {
      const next = await env.mineWhile(scanner.next());
      if (next.done) break;
      await env.run(next.value.ack());
      seen.push(next.value);
    }
    const txs = seen.flatMap((e) => (e.type === 'block' ? e.transactions : []));
    expect(
      txs.map((t) =>
        t.transfers.map((tr) =>
          tr.unresolved ? tr.unresolved.code : tr.amount.format(),
        ),
      ),
    ).toEqual([['0.000004 TRX'], ['0.000002 USDT'], ['ASSET_RESOLUTION']]);
    expect(txs[2]).toMatchObject({
      decoding: 'partial',
      transfers: [
        { unresolved: { asset: { standard: 'trc20', contract: JUNK }, amount: 5n } },
      ],
    });
  });

  it('hands the handle its own native TronWeb client', async () => {
    const env = createTronEnv();
    const client = await env.run(native(env.bc, 'tronweb'));
    expect(await env.run(native(env.bc, 'tronweb'))).toBe(client);
    expect(await env.run(client.trx.getBalance(env.address))).toBe(1_000_000_000);
    await env.run(env.aio.close());
  });
});
```

**Review points:**
- Crash tests use `restart({ killPrevious: true })` through `fenceGeneration` (R20, A5), assert the crash-boundary state (`signed`, in the pool or not) and that the signer ran once; `recover()` rebroadcasts the stored raw, never re-signs.
- The expiry test reaches `expired` only through proofs (the node keeps the transaction pooled, never includes it, then drops it at expiry); `rebuild` signs a new Attempt with a new txID and the original is never signed again.
- Lesson 7: the `no-log` token and the `OUT_OF_ENERGY` transfer both end `failed` (`TX_REVERTED`), never `final`; the `fee` token ends `final`/`executed` (the clarified rule: from the sender to the recipient, any amount).
- The never-activated test asserts `exists` is false before the transfer, so it exercises the no-account path.
- The scan emits `final` blocks only, finds the TRX and USDT deposits, and turns the junk token into an `UnresolvedTransfer` without failing the read (R35).

### Task 11: Opt-in read-only integration test

**Files:**
- Test: `test/integration/tron.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: the public API; `test/setup.ts` keeps the real `fetch` only when `CRYPTO_AIO_INTEGRATION=1`.
- Produces: a suite that is skipped by default and, when enabled, checks a live Tron network: `ready()` (the block-0 identity check and the height probe), heights with the solidified height at or below the head, the solidified block's id, a balance, and a TRX fee estimate. Routing comes from `CRYPTO_AIO_IT_TRON_NETWORK` and `CRYPTO_AIO_IT_TRON_URL` (D17); no variable carries a key, and a URL is wrapped in `secret()` anyway.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the test**

`test/integration/tron.test.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * Opt-in, read-only checks against a live Tron network (spec §17), skipped unless
 * CRYPTO_AIO_INTEGRATION=1. Environment variables carry flags and routing only, never keys:
 * - CRYPTO_AIO_IT_TRON_NETWORK: `mainnet`, `shasta` or `nile` (default `nile`);
 * - CRYPTO_AIO_IT_TRON_URL: an endpoint base URL serving `/wallet`, `/walletsolidity` and
 *   `/jsonrpc` (default: the `public` preset, TronGrid without a key).
 */
import { CryptoAio, secret, type ProviderRef } from '../../src';

const enabled = process.env.CRYPTO_AIO_INTEGRATION === '1';
const network = (process.env.CRYPTO_AIO_IT_TRON_NETWORK ?? 'nile') as
  'mainnet' | 'shasta' | 'nile';
const url = process.env.CRYPTO_AIO_IT_TRON_URL;
const provider: ProviderRef = url ? { endpoints: [{ url: secret(url) }] } : 'public';
/** The black-hole account, which exists on every Tron network. */
const BLACK_HOLE = 'T9yD14Nj9j7xAB4dbGeiX9h8unkKHxuWwb';
const suite = enabled ? describe : describe.skip;

suite(`Tron integration on ${network}`, () => {
  it('checks block 0, then reads heights, a solidified block, a balance and a fee', async () => {
    const aio = new CryptoAio({ env: false });
    try {
      const bc = aio.blockchain({ chain: 'tron', network, provider });
      await bc.ready();
      const status = await bc.getNetworkStatus();
      expect(status.height).toBeGreaterThan(0n);
      expect(status.finalizedHeight).toBeGreaterThan(0n);
      expect(status.finalizedHeight).toBeLessThanOrEqual(status.height);
      const block = await bc.getBlock(status.finalizedHeight);
      expect(block?.hash).toMatch(/^[0-9a-f]{64}$/);
      expect((await bc.getBalance(BLACK_HOLE)).amount.asset.id).toBe(
        `tron:${network}/native`,
      );
      const fee = await bc.estimateFee({
        to: BLACK_HOLE,
        amount: 1n,
        from: 'TNPeeaaFB7K9cmo4uQpcU32zGK8G1NYqeL',
      });
      expect(fee.kind).toBe('tron');
      expect(fee.charges[0]?.label).toBe('bandwidth');
    } finally {
      await aio.close();
    }
  }, 60_000);
});
```

````markdown
`Web3` instance, both wired to the same transport. Import `crypto-aio/evm` once to type them.

### Tron networks

The Tron family serves the `tron` chain on `mainnet`, `shasta` and `nile` with tronweb 6
(`npm install tronweb`). Transfers move TRX or a TRC-20 token, with an optional memo.

- **Addresses.** Base58check `T…` is canonical; `address.format({ hex: true })` gives the
  `41…` hex form, and both are accepted as input. Derive keys at `m/44'/195'/0'/0/<i>`
  (SLIP-44 coin type 195) through the wallet's `keyRef.path`.
- **Fees (`tron`).** Every charge is in TRX and may be 0 when staked or free resources cover
  it: `bandwidth` (the transaction's size), `energy` (TRC-20 only), `activation` (a TRX
  transfer to an address that was never activated: 1 TRX, plus 0.1 TRX when you have no
  staked bandwidth) and `memo` (1 TRX today). Prices come from the chain's parameters on
  every estimate, and the charges are an `upper` bound. TRC-20 energy is simulated and gets
  a 20% margin (`chains.tron.options.energyMarginPercent`); the transaction's `feeLimit`
  covers all of it, because Tron caps energy by `feeLimit` even when it comes from staked
  TRX. The only override is `{ feeLimit }` in sun (`TronFeeOverride`), on TRC-20 transfers,
  never below the estimate: a lower cap fails on chain and still pays.
- **Expiry, not replacement.** A transaction expires 60 s after its reference block
  (`chains.tron.options.expirationMs`, from 10 s to 5 minutes). Tron has no replace and no
  cancel. A transaction that never lands is proven `expired` once a solidified block passes
  its expiration and a scan of the blocks that could hold it shows it absent; then
  `bc.rebuild(id)` re-issues it. Offline signers must sign within the window.
- **Finality.** `final` is the solidified block, about 19 blocks (a minute) below the head.
  Credit deposits on `final`.
- **Tokens.** TRC-20. USDT is registered by alias on mainnet; any other token resolves by
  contract (`{ standard: 'trc20', contract: 'T…' }`), with its decimals read from the chain.
  A token transfer counts as executed only with its `Transfer` event from the token
  contract; TRC-10 transfers are not decoded.
- **Memos** are UTF-8, at most 256 bytes, public forever, and cost the memo fee.
- **Scanning and history.** Blocks carry TRX transfers and TRC-20 `Transfer` events; any
  contract call is `decoding: 'partial'`. Address history comes from TronGrid: name the
  `trongrid` or `public` preset as the handle's `indexer`. It lists the account's own
  transactions first, then the TRC-20 transfers it only received.
- **Presets and endpoints.** `trongrid` sends your key in the `TRON-PRO-API-KEY` header;
  `public` is TronGrid without a key, rate limited and not for production (mainnet refuses
  sustained keyless use). An endpoint of your own must serve `/wallet`, `/walletsolidity`
  and `/jsonrpc` under one base URL, as TronGrid does; a bare java-tron node serves them on
  separate ports, so put a reverse proxy in front of it.
- **Identical payouts.** Tron has no nonce. Each build gets a unique timestamp and
  expiration, so two identical transfers built in the same millisecond still differ.
- **Proven verdicts need two providers.** `trongrid` and `public` are one endpoint each, so
  the proof quorum is 1: a lagging backend decides nothing, but a wrong one is trusted. For
  proven verdicts on mainnet, configure two or more independent providers, for example
  `provider: ['tron', 'own-node']`.
- **After a refusal.** A node remembers a transaction id it refused, so resending the same
  bytes to it answers "duplicate". After a top-up, resend through another endpoint, or wait
  for the proven expiry and `rebuild`.
- **Custom endpoints.** A custom `indexer` endpoint must also answer `/wallet/getblockbynum`
  and `/wallet/getblock` (its health probes). `crypto-aio/tron` names tronweb's types, so a
  project with `skipLibCheck: false` needs tronweb installed to import it.

```ts
import { Blockchain, configure, localSigner, secret, type TronFeeOverride } from 'crypto-aio';
import { native } from 'crypto-aio/native';
import 'crypto-aio/tron';

configure({
  providers: {
    tron: { preset: 'trongrid', apiKey: secret(process.env.TRONGRID_API_KEY ?? '') },
  },
  signers: { hot: localSigner({ secp256k1: secret(process.env.TRON_HOT_KEY ?? '') }) },
  wallets: { 'tron-hot': { signer: 'hot' } },
  chains: {
    tron: {
      network: 'mainnet',
      provider: 'tron',
      indexer: 'tron', // address history (TronGrid /v1)
      wallet: 'tron-hot',
      options: { expirationMs: 120_000 }, // default 60_000, at most 300_000
    },
  },
});
const tron = Blockchain.create({ chain: 'tron' });
const fee = await tron.estimateFee({ asset: 'USDT', to, amount: '25' });
const sub = await tron.transfer(
  { asset: 'USDT', to, amount: '25', memo: 'order 7' },
  { idempotencyKey: 'withdrawal-42' },
);
await sub.wait({ finality: 'final' }); // the solidified block
const cap: TronFeeOverride = { feeLimit: 30_000_000n }; // sun; never below the estimate
const { energy } = await tron.ext.tron.getResources(to);
const client = await native(tron, 'tronweb'); // a TronWeb on the same transport
```
````

## Chain-specific risks

Each Tron pitfall, and how this plan handles it.

| Risk | What goes wrong | How the plan handles it | Pinned in |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Phantom TRC-20 success (lesson 7) | A transfer is included with `contractRet` SUCCESS but moved nothing (a token that logs nothing), or USDT's zero return value is read as success | Verdicts on our own Attempts require `receipt.result == SUCCESS` **and** a `Transfer` log from the called contract, from the sender to the recipient (D12); a status lookup by id and general decoding stay chain-faithful (lesson 15) | Tasks 6, 8, 10 |
| Fee-on-transfer tokens | Tron USDT has a dormant fee switch (`basisPointsRate`); with a fee on, the recipient's `Transfer` carries less than the call's amount | The verdict does not match the amount (any positive amount counts); decoding reports what moved | Tasks 6, 10 |
| Amounts above 2^53 sun | `JSON.parse` and tronweb round u64 amounts; a large balance or deposit would be misread, retried forever or dropped | `exactIntegers` on every java-tron call (A12, Task 0); TRX amounts decoded from the bytes; building above 2^53 − 1 fails closed (D20) | Tasks 0, 2, 4, 6 |
| Single-endpoint proof quorum | Both presets are one URL each, so `proof.quorum` = 1: a lagging backend decides nothing, but a wrong one is trusted | The guide recommends two or more independent providers for proven verdicts; A14 (Plan 2.5) keeps a height liar from shrinking a larger quorum | Task 12; Plan 2.5 |
| `feeLimit` too low | Included, fails `OUT_OF_ENERGY`, still burns TRX | `fee_limit` covers all simulated energy plus a 20% margin (staked energy counts against it); overrides below the estimate are refused; a later energy rise is proven `failed`, never `final` | Tasks 5, 7, 8, 10 |
| Energy or bandwidth shortfall | A transaction signed that the account cannot pay for | `estimateFee` reads resources and chain parameters; `checkFunds` needs TRX for every charge (and the token amount for TRC-20) before signing | Tasks 5, 7, 10 |
| Never-activated sender | No account: no free bandwidth, "no OwnerAccount" | Balance 0 and no free bandwidth ⇒ `INSUFFICIENT_FUNDS` before signing; a broadcast refusal maps to `INSUFFICIENT_FUNDS` (`refused`) | Tasks 5, 7, 10 |
| Sending TRX to an unactivated address | 1 TRX creation fee in the system contract, plus 0.1 TRX when no staked bandwidth (free bandwidth never applies) | An `activation` charge and the creation-bandwidth rule in the estimate and the funds check | Tasks 5, 7, 10 |
| Expiry versus reference-block staleness | A stale head gives a transaction born nearly expired; an orphaned reference fails TaPoS | The head must be younger than half the window (D4); a TaPoS failure is `refused` and the Attempt dies by proven expiry, then `rebuild` | Tasks 5, 7, 10 |
| Solidity-node and TronGrid lag (lesson 16) | "Not found" from a lagging index would prove a live transfer expired, and `rebuild` would pay twice | "Not included" only after attested expiry plus a block scan of the whole window (D3); an index miss for a transaction a scanned block holds decides nothing | Task 8 |
| Moving solidified head (lesson 17) | Two honest endpoints rarely agree on "the latest solidified block" | Predicate keys and fixed heights read solidity-scoped; no endpoint proposes a verdict height; `finalizedHead` trails by 2 blocks (D19) | Tasks 4, 8 |
| A lying endpoint in the negative scan | Old block timestamps would stop the scan before the transaction's block | The JSON-RPC block key compares `timestamp` (lesson 2); pinned by a liar test | Tasks 4, 8 |
| A partial endpoint | A URL without `/walletsolidity` or `/jsonrpc` passes a one-path identity check, then every proof stalls | The rpc identity probe requires one block-0 id from all three services (D2) | Task 8 |
| No nonce: identical transactions | Two payouts built alike share one txID and one effect | Unique timestamp and expiration per build (D5) | Task 7 |
| Broadcast answers in HTTP 200 bodies | The transport cannot mark a retried, delivered broadcast ambiguous | `rejected` only for texts identical on every node and time; everything else non-terminal; node-local codes thrown as possibly sent (D10) | Task 5 |
| Signature accepted per account permissions | A valid signature is refused after a permission change (multi-signature) | `SIGERROR` beyond a wrong size is `refused`, never `rejected` | Task 5 |
| Dynamic energy | USDT's energy rises under load after the estimate | The margin; the proven `failed` path when it is not enough | Tasks 5, 10 |
| Memo | Public forever; costs 1 TRX | Bounded to 256 bytes, charged as `memo`, classified sensitive, never logged; the guide warns | Tasks 5, 7, 12 |
| Protobuf integer precision | tronweb encodes amounts as JS numbers (int64 above 2^53 rounds) | `encodeRaw` refuses unsafe integers; `decodeRaw` refuses bytes it cannot re-encode identically | Task 2 |
| Lenient SDK decoding (lesson 4) | tronweb hashes any-length "public key", drops `ref_block_num` | SDK-free strict addresses (R58); strict and lenient raw decoding kept apart | Task 2 |
| Wrong network (lesson 5) | A Shasta endpoint behind a mainnet handle | Every endpoint must serve block 0's id first (spec §11); `assemble` only signs bytes it built, whose TaPoS reference comes from the checked endpoint | Task 8 |

## Unresolved assumptions

Each item could not be verified or decided beyond doubt; the default chosen and its cost if wrong.

- **One base URL for `/wallet`, `/walletsolidity` and `/jsonrpc` (D2).** True for TronGrid on all three networks (checked live). A bare java-tron node serves them on separate ports. Default: document a reverse proxy, and make the rpc identity probe read block 0 from all three services. Cost if wrong for a user's provider: the endpoint is never healthy (a loud `ready()` failure), never a stalled monitor.
- **JSON-RPC by hash returns canonical blocks only.** The negative scan reads `eth_getBlockByHash` along parent hashes of an attested solidified block; java-tron's block store holds the applied chain. Default: trust the block-by-hash answer under the proof quorum. Cost if wrong: none for soundness below a solidified block (a solidified block's ancestors are final and a hash commits to the header); an endpoint that cannot serve them decides nothing.
- **The scanned window.** Soundness needs every Attempt's expiration window to be at most `MAX_EXPIRATION_MS` (5 minutes). The driver enforces it twice: `tronNetworkConfig` bounds `expirationMs`, and `build` refuses a larger window with `CONFIG_INVALID`. Default: never lower the constant. Cost if someone lowers it: a proof could miss an early inclusion.
- **`getMaxFeeLimit`, energy and bandwidth prices** are read per estimate; the scripted node's values are today's mainnet ones (1,000 and 100 sun). Cost if a proposal changes the formula (for example, a new resource): estimates may be low and the funds check may pass while the node refuses (`refused`, non-terminal).
- **The 20% energy margin** is a policy (D7). Under a steep dynamic-energy rise it can be too little. Cost: a proven `failed` transfer that burned its fee; configurable with `energyMarginPercent`.
- **Keyless TronGrid on testnets** worked during authoring (Nile, Shasta); mainnet answered HTTP 429 without a key. TronGrid documents testnet key policy as subject to change. Cost: the `public` preset may be refused on a testnet; use `trongrid` with a key.
- **java-tron's `Too big transaction` text** (constant 512,000 bytes) is the only size text classed `rejected`; the "new account" variant with its configurable limit is `refused`. Cost: none known.
- **Plan 2's final shapes** (the `crypto-aio/<family>` entry, `fenceGeneration`, the guide texts Task 12 edits) are taken from the Plan 2 plan, its Task 10 report and the controller's rulings, not from merged code. Cost: small edits at execution; see Merge notes.
- **Plan 2.5.** Tasks 1–12 assume Task 0 (`exactIntegers` on `http`), A14, A15 and A17 have landed. Without Task 0, `TronApi` would pass an unknown option (ignored) and amounts above 2^53 would fail as malformed (retryable), as before this review.
- **Proof soundness and the quorum.** Proofs assume Plan 2.5's A14 (height exclusion never shrinks a proof quorum) and, in adversarial settings, at least two independent providers. With one TronGrid URL a lagging backend decides nothing (the scan), but a wrong one is trusted. The guide says so.
- **USDT deprecation.** Tron USDT can `deprecate` and forward to `upgradedAddress`; its `Transfer` log would then come from the new contract, so every family's emitter check (Plan 2's `log.address === tx.to` too) would report `failed` for transfers that moved. Default: keep the emitter check (it blocks phantom logs from other contracts). Cost if it happens: false `failed` verdicts on USDT until the catalog and the check follow the upgrade. Posted to the board.
- **The in-block expiration rule.** With `getConsensusLogicOptimization` = 1 (mainnet), java-tron also refuses in a block an expiration before the next slot. It only narrows inclusion, so D3 stays sound; the scripted node models it.

## Merge notes

Plan 2 merges into `main` before this plan runs; family plans then integrate one at a time. This plan touches these shared files:

| File | This plan's change | How it combines |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `package.json` | tronweb `6.5.1` in `devDependencies`; `"tronweb": "^6.5.1"` in `peerDependencies` with `peerDependenciesMeta.tronweb.optional`; the `"./tron"` export and `typesVersions.tron` (Tasks 2, 9) | Add next to Plan 2's ethers/web3 (and Plan 3's entries if merged first); keep keys alphabetical; `dependencies` unchanged |
| `pnpm-lock.yaml` | tronweb's tree (Task 2) | Regenerate with `pnpm install` under pnpm 10.5.2 on top of the merged lockfile; never hand-merge. tronweb pins `ethers` 6.17.0, the version Plan 2 pins |
| `src/index.ts` | Type-only exports of `TronExt`, `TronFeeDetails`, `TronFeeOverride`, `TronResources` (Task 1); `import { tronPlugin }` and `tronPlugin()` in `BUILTIN_PLUGINS` (Task 9) | Append after Plan 2's EVM export and `evmPlugin()`; order in `BUILTIN_PLUGINS` does not matter (plugins own disjoint chains) |
| `typedoc.json` | `src/adapters/tron/index.ts` in `entryPoints` (Task 9) | Append after `src/adapters/evm/index.ts` |
| `test/architecture/registry-augmentation.test.ts` | The `'crypto-aio/tron'` path and a two-test Tron block (Task 9) | Appended after Plan 2's EVM block; independent of other families' blocks |
| `docs/guides/*.md`, `README.md`, `CHANGELOG.md` | Tron rows, a "Tron networks" section, notes (Task 12) | Written against Plan 2 Task 13's texts; if another family merged first and rewrote a sentence, add the Tron clause to its current text (Task 12 says so) |
| `src/testing/generation.ts` | Consumed, not changed (Task 10) | Created by Plan 2 Task 11 (ruling A5) |
| `src/core/util/json.ts`, `src/core/transport/{types,http-transport}.ts`, `test/core/transport/exact-integers.test.ts` | Task 0: `exactIntegers` on `http` | Lifted into Plan 2.5 (A11, A12); it merges with Plan 5's P5-A (`rpc`/`rpcRaw`) there |
| The SDK-free main-entry guard (R82) | `'tronweb'` added to its unresolvable SDKs (Task 9) | Only if Plan 2's guard has landed; append to its list |

No change to `src/core/**`, `src/testing/**`, error codes or capability names beyond Task 0 (lifted into Plan 2.5). Rebase: after Plan 2 **and Plan 2.5** (and any other family) merge, rebase this branch onto `main`, drop Task 0 (it is in `main`), rerun `pnpm install`, then `pnpm format && pnpm lint && pnpm typecheck && pnpm test && pnpm build && pnpm doc`.

Once Plan 2.5 adds `reason?` to `includedFinal`'s included branch (A9, P6-2), return `...(verdict.reason ? { reason: verdict.reason } : {})` from Tron's `includedFinal` (Task 8), so a proven failure carries its fixed reason.

| Transaction info | TRC-20: `receipt.result`, `log[]` (emitter without `41`), `contractResult` (USDT returns a zero word); TRX: no `receipt.result`; failure adds `result: FAILED` | live `gettransactioninfobyid` on mainnet |
| Solidity paths | `/wallet/getblock` and `/walletsolidity/getblock` (`id_or_num`, `detail`) come from separate HTTP services in java-tron | java-tron `FullNodeHttpApiService`, `HttpApiOnSolidityService` |
| tronweb | 6.5.1 (latest, 2026-09-16); `txJsonToPb` + `txPbToRawDataHex` re-encoded 283 of 285 mainnet Transfer/TriggerSmartContract transactions byte for byte (the 2 carried `ref_block_num`, which tronweb drops); signatures are r ‖ s ‖ (recovery + 27); `computeAddress` hashes non-65-byte input as is | `npm view tronweb`; authoring checks |

## Appendix B: Left out, because it could not be verified or is out of scope

- **USDC on Tron.** Circle's USDC contract-address page lists no Tron deployment; no USDC alias is registered.
- **A public mainnet endpoint other than TronGrid.** Tron's docs ask not to use the seed nodes' HTTP APIs for queries; `api.nileex.io` serves Nile's `/wallet` and `/walletsolidity` but not `/jsonrpc`, so it cannot serve the proofs. The `public` preset is keyless TronGrid on every network.
- **Per-network block-time guarantees beyond the 3-second slot.** `maxLagBlocks` and `reorgWindow` are library policies (D7).
- **TRC-10, multi-signature permission flows, staking and delegation.** Out of scope (the spec lists TRC-20 only; D18).
